			<script>
				// adding this dummy function here to avoid page break of buy/internet page. that page is calling this function
			function unmountGlobalNavScripts() {
				console.log('unmountGlobalNavScripts called latest..');
				const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
				if (isNativeApp) {
				const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
				gnavHeaderId?.remove();
				}
			}
			function getMeganavCookie(lookup) {
				let nameEQ = 'accessDomain' + '=';
				const ca = document.cookie.split(';');
				if (typeof lookup !== 'undefined') {
				nameEQ = lookup + '=';
				}
				for (let c of ca) {
				while (c.charAt(0) === ' ') {
					c = c.substring(1, c.length);
				}
				if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) === 0) {
					return c.substring(nameEQ.length, c.length);
				}
				}
			}
			const isNativeApp = getMeganavCookie('accessDomain') === 'native';
			if (isNativeApp) {
				const gnavHeaderId = document.getElementById('gnavSSR') || document.querySelector('[data-mn-header]');
				gnavHeaderId?.remove();
			} else {
				// Process scripts based on the nav version
				document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
				let scripts = document.querySelectorAll(
					'script[src*="/nav"][type="text/plain"]',
				);
				console.log('@@@ [LOAD_SCRIPTS] scripts:', scripts);
				if (scripts && scripts.length > 0) {
					scripts.forEach((script) => {
					const newScript = document.createElement('script');

					newScript.type = 'module';
					newScript.setAttribute('defer', '');
					newScript.src = script.src;

					console.log('@@@ [LOAD_SCRIPTS] Replacing script:', script.src);
					script.parentNode.replaceChild(newScript, script);
					});
				}
				});
			}
			</script>

				<script id="meganav-header-script">
					(function () {
				const linkPositionKey = {
				footer: 'Footer',
				};
				const footerAbortController = new AbortController();
				const footerSignal = footerAbortController.signal;
				const FOOTER_WRAPPER_SELECTOR = '[data-mn-footer-wrapper]';
				const SOCIAL_LINK_SELECTOR = '[data-mn-footer-social-link]';
				const RESOURCES_LINK_SELECTOR = '[data-mn-footer-resource-link]';
				const LEGAL_LINK_SELECTOR = '[data-mn-footer-legal-link]';
				const REPORTING_BOUND_ATTR = 'data-mn-reporting-bound';
				const COPYRIGHT_TEXT_ATTR = '[data-mn-footer-copyright-text]';
				const eventActionsEnum = {
				LINK_CLICK: 'linkClick'
				};

				const fireLinkClickEvent = (
				linkName,
				linkPath,
				linkPosition,
				linkDestinationUrl,
				) => {
				console.log('mega-nav: fireLinkClickEvent: Firing Link_Click event for linkName:', linkName, 'linkPath:', linkPath, 'linkPosition:', linkPosition, 'linkDestinationUrl:', linkDestinationUrl);
				if (
					typeof globalThis?.ssaf !== 'undefined' &&
					typeof globalThis?.DataMappingInterface !== 'undefined'
				) {
					const AnalyticsEvent = {
					additionaldata: {
						'events.linkDestinationUrl': linkDestinationUrl,
						'events.linkName': linkName,
						'events.linkPosition': linkPosition,
					},
					datatransform: false,
					eventAction: eventActionsEnum.LINK_CLICK,
					eventCode: 'Link_Click',
					};
					if (linkPath) {
					AnalyticsEvent.additionaldata['events.linkPath'] = linkPath;
					}
					globalThis?.ssaf.sendData(AnalyticsEvent);
				} else {
					console.warn(
					'mega-nav: fireLinkClickEvent: DEDM object ssaf or DataMappingInterface does not exist on globalThis object, ' +
					'no Link_Click report created',
					);
				}
				};

				const isReportingBound = (element) =>
				element.getAttribute(REPORTING_BOUND_ATTR) === 'true';

				const markReportingBound = (element) => {
				element.setAttribute(REPORTING_BOUND_ATTR, 'true');
				};

				const resolveHref = (rawHref) => {
				if (!rawHref) {
					return '';
				}

				if (typeof globalThis.window === 'undefined') {
					return rawHref;
				}

				const normalized = rawHref.toLowerCase();
				if (!/^(http|https)/.test(normalized)) {
					return `${globalThis.window.location.origin}${rawHref}`;
				}

				return rawHref;
				};

				const emitFooterLinkClick = (linkName, linkPath, href) => {
				fireLinkClickEvent(linkName, linkPath, linkPositionKey.footer, href);
				};

				const registerFooterController = () => {
				const wrapper = document.querySelector(FOOTER_WRAPPER_SELECTOR);
				if (!wrapper) {
					return;
				}
				if (wrapper.__mnFooterAbortController) {
					try {
					wrapper.__mnFooterAbortController.abort();
					} catch (e) {}
				}
				wrapper.__mnFooterAbortController = footerAbortController;
				};

				const initSocialReporting = (wrapper, signal) => {
				const links = wrapper.querySelectorAll(SOCIAL_LINK_SELECTOR);

				links.forEach((link) => {
					if (isReportingBound(link)) {
					return;
					}

					const text = (
					link.getAttribute('data-mn-footer-link-text') ||
					link.textContent ||
					''
					).trim();

					link.addEventListener('click', () => {
					const href = resolveHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
					emitFooterLinkClick(text, href, href);
					}, { signal });

					markReportingBound(link);
				});
				};

				const initResourcesReporting = (wrapper, signal) => {
				const links = wrapper.querySelectorAll(RESOURCES_LINK_SELECTOR);

				links.forEach((link) => {
					if (isReportingBound(link)) {
					return;
					}

					const text = (
					link.getAttribute('data-mn-footer-link-text') ||
					link.textContent ||
					''
					).trim();

					link.addEventListener('click', () => {
					const href = resolveHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
					emitFooterLinkClick(text, href, href);
					}, { signal });

					markReportingBound(link);
				});
				};

				const initLegalReporting = (wrapper, signal) => {
				const links = wrapper.querySelectorAll(LEGAL_LINK_SELECTOR);

				links.forEach((link) => {
					if (isReportingBound(link)) {
					return;
					}

					const text = (
					link.getAttribute('data-mn-footer-link-text') ||
					link.textContent ||
					''
					).trim();

					link.addEventListener('click', () => {
					const href = resolveHref(link.getAttribute('href'));
					emitFooterLinkClick(text, href, href);
					}, { signal });

					markReportingBound(link);
				});
				};

				const initCopyrightText = (wrapper) => {
				const copyrightElement = wrapper.querySelector(COPYRIGHT_TEXT_ATTR);

				if (copyrightElement) {
					const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
					const rawCopyright = copyrightElement.textContent || '';
					const updatedCopyright = rawCopyright.replace(/(\d{4})/, currentYear.toString());
					copyrightElement.textContent = updatedCopyright;
				}
				};

				const initFooterReporting = (signal) => {
				const wrapper = document.querySelector(FOOTER_WRAPPER_SELECTOR);

				if (!wrapper) {
					return;
				}

				initSocialReporting(wrapper, signal);
				initResourcesReporting(wrapper, signal);
				initLegalReporting(wrapper, signal);
				initCopyrightText(wrapper);
				};

				function parseIncludedCoBrowsePages(value) {
				try {
					const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
					return Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed.filter(v => typeof v === 'string') : [];
				} catch (e) {
					return [];
				}
				}

				function isCurrentPathIncluded(includedCoBrowsePages) {
				if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
				if (!includedCoBrowsePages) return true;
				let path = window.location.pathname;
				// Remove trailing slashes (except for root)
				if (path.length > 1) path = path.replace(/\/+$/, '');
				const includedPages = parseIncludedCoBrowsePages(includedCoBrowsePages);
				if (!includedPages.length) return true;
				// If only "/" is present, match all paths
				if (includedPages.length === 1 && includedPages[0] === '/') {
					return window.location.hostname.endsWith('att.com');
				}
				for (const basePath of includedPages) {
					if (basePath === '/') {
					if (path === '/') return true;
					} else {
					if (path === basePath || path.startsWith(basePath + '/')) {
						return true;
					}
					}
				}
				return false;
				}

				const showCoBrowse = () => {
				const coBrowseLink = document.querySelector('#coBrowserSixDigitButtonContainer');
				if (coBrowseLink) {
					const mnFeatureFlags = JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('meganav-featureflags') || '{}')?.allocations || {};
					const includedCoBrowsePages = mnFeatureFlags['cfg-globalnav-footer-enable-cobrowse-link-pages']?.value || null;
					const includeCoBrowseFooterLink = isCurrentPathIncluded(includedCoBrowsePages);
					if (includeCoBrowseFooterLink) {
					coBrowseLink?.classList?.remove('hide');
					}
				}
				};

				const IXP_CACHE_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags';
				const IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY = 'meganav-featureflags-ts';
				const IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;

				const callIxp = async () => {
				try {
					// Return cached data if still within the 15-min TTL
					const rawFlags = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY);
					const rawTimestamp = sessionStorage.getItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY);
					if (rawFlags && rawTimestamp) {
					const cachedAt = Number(rawTimestamp);
					if (Number.isFinite(cachedAt) && Date.now() - cachedAt < IXP_CACHE_TTL_MS) {
						const parsed = JSON.parse(rawFlags);
						if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object') {
						return parsed;
						}
					}
					}

					console.log('IXP CSR call from footer !!');
					const customHeaders = new Headers({
					Accept: 'application/json',
					'Content-Type': 'application/json',
					});
					const res = await fetch('/msapi/ixp-allocation-manager-service/v1/allocations?app=idp-globalnav', { headers: customHeaders });
					if (res.ok) {
					const resD = await res.json();
					sessionStorage.setItem(IXP_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(resD));
					sessionStorage.setItem(IXP_CACHE_TS_KEY, String(Date.now()));
					return resD;
					} else {
					console.error('Error response from IXP allocation manager service at csr:', res.status, res.statusText);
					return null;
					}
				} catch (e) {
					console.error('Error in ixp fetch at csr:', e);
					return null;
				}
				};

				const tryInit = async () => {
				console.log('mega-nav: Footer: Initializing footer scripts');

				registerFooterController();

				// call IXP from footer only when footer is rendered without header, which means header script is not called. This is to cover the scenario where consumer directly embeds footer without header.
				const header = document.querySelector('#meganavNXUILayoutsmb_header, #meganavNXUILayoutconsumer_header');
				let ixpValues;
				if (!header) {
					ixpValues = await callIxp();
				}
				showCoBrowse();
				initFooterReporting(footerSignal);
				const mnFeatureFlags = ixpValues?.allocations || {};
				if (mnFeatureFlags['svc-meganav-dispatch-footer-loaded-enabled']?.value === 'true') {
					// this custom event is dispatched for cobrowse script to listen and bind click event on cobrowse link when footer is rerendered without page reload
					// this event will be dispatched on page load as well but cobrowse script will not be present at that time to listen to this event, so it will not cause any issue
					window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('mnfooterloaded', {}));
				}
				
				};

				function runOnDomReady(fn) {
				if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
					document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fn, { signal: footerSignal });
				} else {
					fn();
				}
				}
				runOnDomReady(tryInit);
			})();
				</script>
				<style>
					a.footer-social-link>svg {
						vertical-align: middle;
					}

					a.footer-social-link {
						height: 34px;
						width: 34px;
						transition: 0.3s ease;
					}
